This feature supplies the single predicate that decides whether a value is null, so counts, typing, statistics, and frequency lists agree.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- Package `internal/profile` exports `IsNull(value string) bool`.\n- The value is trimmed of leading and trailing spaces before the test, so the predicate gives the same answer for a padded value and its trimmed form.\n- A value is null when the trimmed value is empty, or equals `NA`, `N/A`, or `NULL` ignoring case (`na`, `n/a`, `Null`, `null` are null).\n- Every other value is non-null, including near misses such as `NAN`, `N/B`, `NULLS`, `none`, and `0`.\n- The predicate is pure: no I/O, no package state.\n\n## Required project tests\n\nThe builder writes these tests in `internal/profile` beside the code, with exactly these names:\n\n- `TestIsNullEmpty`: the empty string and whitespace-only strings are null.\n- `TestIsNullSpellings`: `NA`, `N/A`, `NULL` in upper, lower, and mixed case are null, including when padded with spaces.\n- `TestIsNullNonNull`: near misses and ordinary values are not null.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC nullvalues-empty ===\nIntent: Empty and whitespace-only values are classified as null by the delivered predicate.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/profile\", \"TestIsNullEmpty\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC nullvalues-empty ===\n\n=== AC nullvalues-spellings ===\nIntent: NA, N/A, and NULL are null in any letter case, padded or not.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/profile\", \"TestIsNullSpellings\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC nullvalues-spellings ===\n\n=== AC nullvalues-nonnull ===\nIntent: Near misses and ordinary values are not null.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: executable=go; This feature parses them and turns every usage mistake into exit code 2 with nothing on standard output.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- `internal/cli.Run(args []string, stdout, stderr io.Writer) int` parses `args` with a local `flag.FlagSet` created with `flag.ContinueOnError`, its own output set to `io.Discard`.\n- Options precede the file. Exactly one positional argument, the file, is required. Anything after the first positional argument counts as an extra argument.\n- Recognized options: `--column <name>`, `--freq <name>`, `--top <n>`.\n- Exit 2 with empty standard output for each of:\n  - no file, or more than one file;\n  - an unknown option;\n  - an option given without its value;\n  - `--column` together with `--freq`;\n  - `--top` without `--freq`.\n- On exit 2, write exactly one line to standard error naming both command forms. There is no `--help` option.\n- A well-formed command line is accepted and handed to the later behavior; it never exits 2.\n- A `--top` value that is not a positive integer is owned by the `--top` feature (FEATURE-FrequencyTop.md), not this one.\n\n## Required project tests\n\nThe builder writes tests calling `cli.Run` with `bytes.Buffer` writers for every case above, asserting the exit code, empty standard output, and a single-line diagnostic.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC arguments-file-count ===\nIntent: A missing file or an extra file exits 2 with nothing on standard output.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: executable=go; This feature creates the typing function and implements its first two rungs; later features add the remaining rungs to the same function.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- Package `internal/profile` exports `InferType(values []string) string`. Values are already trimmed; null values are skipped using `IsNull`.\n- Returns `\"integer\"` when every non-null value matches `^-?[0-9]+$`.\n- Returns `\"number\"` when every non-null value matches `^-?[0-9]+(\\.[0-9]+)?$` and at least one value is not an integer.\n- Integer text with leading zeros or a leading `-` and digits is still an integer; `+5`, `.5`, `5.`, `1e3`, `1,000`, and a bare `-` are not numeric.\n- Typing considers only non-null values: a column of integers and nulls is `integer`.\n- The ladder order is integer, number, date, boolean, text; a column that satisfies an earlier rung never takes a later one.\n- Non-numeric columns fall through to later rungs, implemented by FEATURE-TypeDate.md and FEATURE-TypeOther.md. Until they exist, a non-numeric column returns `\"text\"`.\n\n## Required project tests\n\nThe builder writes these tests in `internal/profile` with exactly these names:\n\n- `TestInferTypeInteger`: integer columns, negative values, leading zeros, and integers mixed with nulls.\n- `TestInferTypeNumber`: decimals, negative decimals, decimals mixed with integers, and decimals mixed with nulls.\n- `TestInferTypeNotNumeric`: forms such as `+5`, `.5`, `5.`, `1e3`, `1,000`, and `-` are not integer or number.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC typenumeric-integer ===\nIntent: Integer columns, including negatives and nulls, are typed integer by the delivered function.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/profile\", \"TestInferTypeInteger\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC typenumeric-integer ===\n\n=== AC typenumeric-number ===\nIntent: Columns with at least one decimal value and otherwise numeric values are typed number.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/profile\", \"TestInferTypeNumber\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC typenumeric-number ===\n\n=== AC typenumeric-not-numeric ===\nIntent: Malformed numeric spellings never type a column as integer or number.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: executable=go; A value such as `2025-02-30` has the right shape but is not a date, so it keeps its column out of this type.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- `InferType` returns `\"date\"` when the column is not integer or number and every non-null value matches `^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}$` and parses with `time.Parse(\"2006-01-02\", value)` without error.\n- The shape check is strict: the value must be exactly ten characters in that form; a single-digit month or day, a time suffix such as `2025-03-01T08:00`, or a slash separator is not a date.\n- Calendar validity includes month length and leap years: `2024-02-29` is a date, `2025-02-29` and `2025-04-31` are not.\n- A single invalid date among valid ones sends the column to the later rungs (boolean, text), so it becomes `text`.\n- Null values are ignored.\n- A column that is entirely integers or numbers keeps that earlier type.\n\n## Required project tests\n\nThe builder writes these tests in `internal/profile` with exactly these names:\n\n- `TestInferTypeDate`: valid dates, leap day, and dates mixed with nulls are typed date.\n- `TestInferTypeInvalidDate`: impossible calendar dates, wrong shapes, and time suffixes make the column text.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC typedate-valid ===\nIntent: Columns of real calendar dates, including a leap day and nulls, are typed date.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/profile\", \"TestInferTypeDate\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC typedate-valid ===\n\n=== AC typedate-invalid ===\nIntent: One impossible date or malformed shape keeps a column from being typed date.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/profile\", \"TestInferTypeInvalidDate\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC typedate-invalid ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- Calendar validity is decided by the standard library, not by a hand-written month table.\n- Typing never reads a data file and never hardcodes a value.\n- Standard library only.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-TypeOther.md ===\n# FEATURE: Type Other\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261025 V1 |\n| Description | Infers the boolean, text, and empty column types. |\n| Depends On  | FEATURE-TypeNumeric.md |\n| Provides    | boolean, text, empty types |\n| Consumes    | internal/profile.InferType |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nThis feature completes the typing ladder: `boolean` for true/false columns, `text` as the catch-all, and `empty` for columns with no non-null value.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- `InferType` returns `\"empty\"` when no value in the column is non-null. This includes a column with no rows at all, a column of only empty strings, and a column of only `NA`, `N/A`, or `NULL` spellings.\n- `InferType` returns `\"boolean\"` when the column is not integer, number, or date, and every non-null value equals `true` or `false` ignoring case (`TRUE`, `False`).\n- `InferType` returns `\"text\"` for every other column, including a column mixing booleans with other words, or booleans with numbers.\n- Null values are ignored for every rung; a column of booleans and nulls is `boolean`.\n- `yes`, `no`, `1`, `0`, `t`, `f` are not booleans.\n- `empty` takes precedence over every other type because it is decided first, by the absence of non-null values.\n\n## Required project tests\n\nThe builder writes these tests in `internal/profile` with exactly these names:\n\n- `TestInferTypeBoolean`: mixed-case true/false columns, with and without nulls.\n- `TestInferTypeText`: ordinary words, mixed booleans and words, padded and punctuated strings.\n- `TestInferTypeEmpty`: no values, only empty strings, only null spellings.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC typeother-boolean ===\nIntent: Columns whose non-null values are all true or false in any case are typed boolean.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/profile\", \"TestInferTypeBoolean\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC typeother-boolean ===\n\n=== AC typeother-text ===\nIntent: Columns that satisfy no earlier rung are typed text.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: executable=go; This feature supplies the single predicate that decides whether a value is null, so counts, typing, statistics, and frequency lists agree.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- Package `internal/profile` exports `IsNull(value string) bool`.\n- The value is trimmed of leading and trailing spaces before the test, so the predicate gives the same answer for a padded value and its trimmed form.\n- A value is null when the trimmed value is empty, or equals `NA`, `N/A`, or `NULL` ignoring case (`na`, `n/a`, `Null`, `null` are null).\n- Every other value is non-null, including near misses such as `NAN`, `N/B`, `NULLS`, `none`, and `0`.\n- The predicate is pure: no I/O, no package state.\n\n## Required project tests\n\nThe builder writes these tests in `internal/profile` beside the code, with exactly these names:\n\n- `TestIsNullEmpty`: the empty string and whitespace-only strings are null.\n- `TestIsNullSpellings`: `NA`, `N/A`, `NULL` in upper, lower, and mixed case are null, including when padded with spaces.\n- `TestIsNullNonNull`: near misses and ordinary values are not null.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC nullvalues-empty ===\nIntent: Empty and whitespace-only values are classified as null by the delivered predicate.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/profile\", \"TestIsNullEmpty\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC nullvalues-empty ===\n\n=== AC nullvalues-spellings ===\nIntent: NA, N/A, and NULL are null in any letter case, padded or not.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/profile\", \"TestIsNullSpellings\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC nullvalues-spellings ===\n\n=== AC nullvalues-nonnull ===\nIntent: Near misses and ordinary values are not null.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/profile\", \"TestIsNullNonNull\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC nullvalues-nonnull ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- Standard library only; no third-party modules.\n- The predicate never reads a data file and never embeds a value taken from a supplied data file.\n- Null spellings are exactly the empty string, `NA`, `N/A`, and `NULL` ignoring case; no other spelling is added.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-Arguments.md ===\n# FEATURE: Arguments\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261025 V1 |\n| Description | Parses the csvstat command line and rejects malformed usage with exit 2. |\n| Depends On  | FEATURE-Build.md |\n| Provides    | --column, --freq, --top, exit code 2 |\n| Consumes    | internal/cli.Run |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nThe command forms are `csvstat [--column <name>] <file>` and `csvstat --freq <name> [--top <n>] <file>`. This feature parses them and turns every usage mistake into exit code 2 with nothing on standard output.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- `internal/cli.Run(args []string, stdout, stderr io.Writer) int` parses `args` with a local `flag.FlagSet` created with `flag.ContinueOnError`, its own output set to `io.Discard`.\n- Options precede the file. Exactly one positional argument, the file, is required. Anything after the first positional argument counts as an extra argument.\n- Recognized options: `--column <name>`, `--freq <name>`, `--top <n>`.\n- Exit 2 with empty standard output for each of:\n  - no file, or more than one file;\n  - an unknown option;\n  - an option given without its value;\n  - `--column` together with `--freq`;\n  - `--top` without `--freq`.\n- On exit 2, write exactly one line to standard error naming both command forms. There is no `--help` option.\n- A well-formed command line is accepted and handed to the later behavior; it never exits 2.\n- A `--top` value that is not a positive integer is owned by the `--top` feature (FEATURE-FrequencyTop.md), not this one.\n\n## Required project tests\n\nThe builder writes tests calling `cli.Run` with `bytes.Buffer` writers for every case above, asserting the exit code, empty standard output, and a single-line diagnostic.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC arguments-file-count ===\nIntent: A missing file or an extra file exits 2 with nothing on standard output.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nimport os\nimport tempfile\n\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\nwith tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:\n    first = os.path.join(d, \"first.csv\")\n    second = os.path.join(d, \"second.csv\")\n    for path in (first, second):\n        with open(path, \"w\", newline=\"\") as f:\n            f.write(\"a,b\\n1,2\\n\")\n\n    code, out, err = cli_runner()\n    print(out)\n    print(err)\n    assert code == 2, (code, out, err)\n    assert out == \"\"\n\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(first, second)\n    print(out)\n    print(err)\n    assert code == 2, (code, out, err)\n    assert out == \"\"\n\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(\"--column\", \"a\")\n    print(out)\n    print(err)\n    assert code == 2, (code, out, err)\n    assert out == \"\"\n=== END AC arguments-file-count ===\n\n=== AC arguments-bad-options ===\nIntent: An unknown option and an option without its value exit 2 with nothing on standard output.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: executable=go; This feature creates the typing function and implements its first two rungs; later features add the remaining rungs to the same function.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- Package `internal/profile` exports `InferType(values []string) string`. Values are already trimmed; null values are skipped using `IsNull`.\n- Returns `\"integer\"` when every non-null value matches `^-?[0-9]+$`.\n- Returns `\"number\"` when every non-null value matches `^-?[0-9]+(\\.[0-9]+)?$` and at least one value is not an integer.\n- Integer text with leading zeros or a leading `-` and digits is still an integer; `+5`, `.5`, `5.`, `1e3`, `1,000`, and a bare `-` are not numeric.\n- Typing considers only non-null values: a column of integers and nulls is `integer`.\n- The ladder order is integer, number, date, boolean, text; a column that satisfies an earlier rung never takes a later one.\n- Non-numeric columns fall through to later rungs, implemented by FEATURE-TypeDate.md and FEATURE-TypeOther.md. Until they exist, a non-numeric column returns `\"text\"`.\n\n## Required project tests\n\nThe builder writes these tests in `internal/profile` with exactly these names:\n\n- `TestInferTypeInteger`: integer columns, negative values, leading zeros, and integers mixed with nulls.\n- `TestInferTypeNumber`: decimals, negative decimals, decimals mixed with integers, and decimals mixed with nulls.\n- `TestInferTypeNotNumeric`: forms such as `+5`, `.5`, `5.`, `1e3`, `1,000`, and `-` are not integer or number.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC typenumeric-integer ===\nIntent: Integer columns, including negatives and nulls, are typed integer by the delivered function.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/profile\", \"TestInferTypeInteger\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC typenumeric-integer ===\n\n=== AC typenumeric-number ===\nIntent: Columns with at least one decimal value and otherwise numeric values are typed number.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/profile\", \"TestInferTypeNumber\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC typenumeric-number ===\n\n=== AC typenumeric-not-numeric ===\nIntent: Malformed numeric spellings never type a column as integer or number.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/profile\", \"TestInferTypeNotNumeric\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC typenumeric-not-numeric ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- Type names are exactly `integer`, `number`, `date`, `boolean`, `text`, and `empty`.\n- Typing never reads a data file and never hardcodes a column name or a type for a column.\n- Standard library only.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-TypeDate.md ===\n# FEATURE: Type Date\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261025 V1 |\n| Description | Infers the date column type with real calendar validation. |\n| Depends On  | FEATURE-TypeNumeric.md |\n| Provides    | date type |\n| Consumes    | internal/profile.InferType |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nA column is a `date` column when every non-null value is a real calendar date written `YYYY-MM-DD`. A value such as `2025-02-30` has the right shape but is not a date, so it keeps its column out of this type.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- `InferType` returns `\"date\"` when the column is not integer or number and every non-null value matches `^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}$` and parses with `time.Parse(\"2006-01-02\", value)` without error.\n- The shape check is strict: the value must be exactly ten characters in that form; a single-digit month or day, a time suffix such as `2025-03-01T08:00`, or a slash separator is not a date.\n- Calendar validity includes month length and leap years: `2024-02-29` is a date, `2025-02-29` and `2025-04-31` are not.\n- A single invalid date among valid ones sends the column to the later rungs (boolean, text), so it becomes `text`.\n- Null values are ignored.\n- A column that is entirely integers or numbers keeps that earlier type.\n\n## Required project tests\n\nThe builder writes these tests in `internal/profile` with exactly these names:\n\n- `TestInferTypeDate`: valid dates, leap day, and dates mixed with nulls are typed date.\n- `TestInferTypeInvalidDate`: impossible calendar dates, wrong shapes, and time suffixes make the column text.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC typedate-valid ===\nIntent: Columns of real calendar dates, including a leap day and nulls, are typed date.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/profile\", \"TestInferTypeDate\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC typedate-valid ===\n\n=== AC typedate-invalid ===\nIntent: One impossible date or malformed shape keeps a column from being typed date.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/profile\", \"TestInferTypeInvalidDate\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC typedate-invalid ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- Calendar validity is decided by the standard library, not by a hand-written month table.\n- Typing never reads a data file and never hardcodes a value.\n- Standard library only.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-TypeOther.md ===\n# FEATURE: Type Other\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261025 V1 |\n| Description | Infers the boolean, text, and empty column types. |\n| Depends On  | FEATURE-TypeNumeric.md |\n| Provides    | boolean, text, empty types |\n| Consumes    | internal/profile.InferType |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nThis feature completes the typing ladder: `boolean` for true/false columns, `text` as the catch-all, and `empty` for columns with no non-null value.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- `InferType` returns `\"empty\"` when no value in the column is non-null. This includes a column with no rows at all, a column of only empty strings, and a column of only `NA`, `N/A`, or `NULL` spellings.\n- `InferType` returns `\"boolean\"` when the column is not integer, number, or date, and every non-null value equals `true` or `false` ignoring case (`TRUE`, `False`).\n- `InferType` returns `\"text\"` for every other column, including a column mixing booleans with other words, or booleans with numbers.\n- Null values are ignored for every rung; a column of booleans and nulls is `boolean`.\n- `yes`, `no`, `1`, `0`, `t`, `f` are not booleans.\n- `empty` takes precedence over every other type because it is decided first, by the absence of non-null values.\n\n## Required project tests\n\nThe builder writes these tests in `internal/profile` with exactly these names:\n\n- `TestInferTypeBoolean`: mixed-case true/false columns, with and without nulls.\n- `TestInferTypeText`: ordinary words, mixed booleans and words, padded and punctuated strings.\n- `TestInferTypeEmpty`: no values, only empty strings, only null spellings.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC typeother-boolean ===\nIntent: Columns whose non-null values are all true or false in any case are typed boolean.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/profile\", \"TestInferTypeBoolean\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC typeother-boolean ===\n\n=== AC typeother-text ===\nIntent: Columns that satisfy no earlier rung are typed text.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/profile\", \"TestInferTypeText\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC typeother-text ===\n\n=== AC typeother-empty ===\nIntent: Columns with no non-null value are typed empty.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/profile\", \"TestInferTypeEmpty\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC typeother-empty ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- Boolean spellings are exactly `true` and `false` ignoring case;
